The startup work is done and verified. Here is the startup report; the Screen 04 result and the specialists' first steps will follow as they finish.

# Startup report (account 12)

I'm not giving the "RAINBOW 3D BACKUP VERIFIED" line, because one startup check failed: the automatic download of your branches at session start (GitHub didn't answer in time). I redid it by hand a few minutes later and it worked, and everything below is verified now.

**Recovered work**
- **Branch:** your branch `ccr-3b139b48-0qqkkz`; its newest commit on GitHub is `5c40dde` (12:29 UTC).
- **This session's branch:** `ccr-e908f8da-cy4yc1`, started from `f06a53e`. Account 11's backup kept running after the switch and added `5c40dde`, which holds only backup logs and no game changes, so nothing was merged.
- **Newest work per screen** comes from the round-11 branches: 05 `2d9fe975`, 08 `35b5fc5a`, 04 `d3ea4200`, 06 `8182130c`, 07 `dbc7678c`. The 05 and 08 workstreams also had unfinished edits saved in `backups/wip/`, which I restored.

**Five-minute autosave: working.**
- The test save `74500f89` reached GitHub with the same commit ID, and the latest save `fb76995` matches too.
- One problem fixed: the autosave settings still pointed at account 11's branch and briefly refused to save. Two saves failed before the fix.
- Each save also backs up the specialists' unfinished edits as clearly labelled snapshots.
- The autosave cannot run while this environment is closed, so I'll do a final save before the session ends.

**Large assets: intact.** All 59 large files are real files here and confirmed on GitHub's large-file storage. All 13 approved reference images are byte-identical, and the three new workspaces have no missing files.

**Specialists: 2 running now**, out of 10 definitions, all present and unchanged.
- **Screen 05** (water-fx-specialist) and **Screen 08** (washer-environment-modeler), each in its own workspace on a new `-c12` branch.
- Account 11's branches are now write-protected, so this account can't overwrite them.
- I'm finishing the Screen 04 test myself.
- Queue: 07 next, then 06. The rejected tunnel-bending method for 06 stays rejected.

**Services: all checked with free reads; nothing was billed.**
- **World Labs Marble:** the key works through this environment, with 5,420 credits, the same account used since round 7. The Screen 08 world opens. The Screen 05 pilot worlds don't open with this key because they belong to a different World Labs account, but their files are safe in the repo. I did not run the Image-Blaster scripts.
- **fal.ai:** your existing account works; saved request `01a1ea70` reads COMPLETED, and all 8 Screen 04 pilot images are in the repo.
- **Hugging Face:** reachable directly, with no connector needed. Stable Virtual Camera and ViewCrafter are still broken on Hugging Face's own servers, and fal doesn't offer them, so they can't be tested today.
- **Depth Anything 3:** the saved results cover all five approved screens. I didn't reinstall it; that takes about 5 minutes and is only needed if a new picture must be measured.
- **Tools:** Blender, Three.js/Vite/TypeScript, Playwright with WebGL2, ffmpeg, the Python image tools and the Android SDK all work, and nothing was reinstalled.

**Needs your action:** nothing.

**Being worked on now**
- **05:** the pale, smeared water just behind the far tunnel section first. Then the torn S-river, the distorted tunnel walls, and the wrong scenery at the 05→06 transition.
- **08:** the spout cups whose tops are cut off by a line first. Then the soft water smears near the start and the seams at the top corners of the drum.
- **04:** the decisive test of projecting your provisional views V04-1 and V04-2 onto the 3D tunnel. V04-1/V04-2 themselves look much closer to your approved painting than the current game: painted gold rings, glowing chevron arrows, thick swirling cyan-and-pink water. In the frames I've seen so far, the projection changes nothing far into the stage and tears patches at the top of the tunnel at 25 units forward. The frames where the views should matter most are still rendering.

The Screen 04 verdict, and the first 05 and 08 results, will come as separate full-size APPROVED / BEFORE / NEW pictures, each labelled GOOD IMPROVEMENT, NOT AN IMPROVEMENT or NOT YET PROVEN.

### 🤖 Claude — 2026-11-18T12:53:28.477Z

Two results so far, one improvement kept and one test rejected:

**Screen 05, step 1: GOOD IMPROVEMENT, modest.** These are the five Screen 05 pictures I just sent.
- **What looks closer:** from two thirds of the way through to the stage end, the river ahead of Benny was a pale lilac smear. It is now a cyan core between hot-pink bands, winding into the glowing tunnel throat as in your painting. Your approved start view is unchanged.
- **Still different:** that far river is flatter than painted (fewer white streaks, no foam clouds on its banks). A straight join line shows where the near water meets it, and the pale slab on the left at two-thirds through is still there.

**Screen 04, view-projection test: NOT AN IMPROVEMENT, rejected.** These are the six Screen 04 pictures I sent first.
- Projecting your provisional views V04-1 and V04-2 onto the tunnel left the game looking the same as before, even at the exact spots where the views were taken. It also added torn gold blocks at the top of the tunnel.
- The views only reached the upper tunnel wall. The water and foam are drawn by other objects, so the painted water and arrows never showed.
- Making it work would need many more paid AI views, and the picture would still tear between them, so I've stopped this method. V04-1 and V04-2 stay as visual guides for rebuilding the real 3D interior.

**Why the five arrows don't show while Benny swims.** In your painting the river climbs toward the far end of the tunnel, so the chevrons stack up the middle of the picture. In the game the camera rides low over almost level water, so flat chevrons shrink to slivers, and Benny's body covers the lane right ahead. Making them thicker didn't help. The real fix is the shape of the water inside the tunnel. I wrote that up as the brief for the next Screen 04 slot, so that work won't repeat these tests.

**Still running:**
- **Screen 05:** next is the join line, then the torn S-river and the scenery at the 05→06 transition.
- **Screen 08:** testing the spout-cup fix left unfinished by account 11, then the water smears and seams. No result yet.

**Queue:** Screen 04 gets the next free slot, then 07, then 06.

Everything is saved: this session's branch is at `b161115` and matches GitHub, and the Screen 04 branch is at `94350998`. I'll show each new result as it arrives.

Do not repeat the same incomplete projection experiment.

2. RECONSTRUCT THE ACTUAL APPROVED SCENERY

My approved Screen 04 has:

- A deep, beautifully polished gold washing-machine tunnel.
- Thick, flowing cyan-blue water with broad pink-magenta swirls.
- Five correctly shaped and positioned glowing arrows.
- Soft white foam integrated along the water edges, not giant white ball walls.
- Small, iridescent bubbles.
- Gold coins following the approved path.
- Benny at the approved scale, position, and appearance, swimming naturally.

The current NEW version has a flat-looking water path, oversized foam, incorrect arrows, and a torn gold column near the top.

FIX THESE ACTUAL DIFFERENCES.

If projection cannot reproduce the water geometry correctly, reconstruct the water as continuous real 3D geometry and use my approved artwork to match its shape, color, and material appearance.

Do not add new scenery or redesign the environment.

3. ONE CONTROLLED TEST — NOT ANOTHER LONG EXPERIMENT

First verify the corrected rendering on the real game at the two existing V04-1 and V04-2 camera positions.

Then test at an intermediate position and while steering.

Only run a larger 12–14-frame audit after the smaller test demonstrates a genuine improvement.

If you cannot reliably achieve one of these, say NO or NOT YET PROVEN. Explain precisely what technical limitation remains and whether there is a realistic AI-only solution.

Do not promise 95% accuracy unless you can demonstrate it in real gameplay.

5. CHECK FOR A GENUINELY BETTER TOOL ONLY IF NECESSARY

### 🤖 Claude — 2026-11-18T13:07:52.403Z

I found and fixed the exact Screen 04 rendering problem. The corrected test works at the two view positions but not between them, so its verdict is **NOT YET PROVEN**. And the honest answer to your main question is: not yet, as explained below.

## 1. The exact rendering problem

I re-drew the real game frame with one object group hidden at a time, which shows which object produces every visible pixel. At the V04-1 position:

- **River (27% of the frame):** a depth-offset mismatch in the code. The river surface was nudged toward the camera more strongly than the overlay drawn on top of it (−4 vs −2), so the game's river won every pixel and the painted water and arrows were discarded. Opacity and masks on the river were fine.
- **Foam (11.5%):** the tall foam-ball sprites are separate objects drawn in front of the painted low foam and water.
- **Torn gold column:** holes in the views where painted coins and bubbles had been cut out; the bare game tunnel showed through.
- **Calibration:** the test frames were taken 0.8–1.1 units past where the views were made. My new test tool now hits the exact spot (0.00 units).

The projection could have supplied 63% of the frame; it actually controlled only 38%, just the upper tunnel walls.

## 2. One controlled test of the corrected version (`&m04mv2=1`)

This fixes all four problems. Your default game and last kept version are untouched; I compared the new pictures against the kept version only by eye, without re-running the default-build check.

| Position | Result |
|---|---|
| Exactly at the V04-1 / V04-2 cameras | **Much closer to your painting:** glossy gold rings, glowing arrows stacked ahead of Benny, thick swirling cyan water with pink-magenta bands, low foam, no torn column. Still wrong: the game's darker water shows as a blob around Benny. |
| Hard steering, near a view | Closer: arrows, glossy rings, painted water; slight ghosting at the top arcs. |
| Halfway between the two views | **Worse: double exposure** (doubled rings, blurry mushed water). |

Because of the halfway result I did not run the 12–14-frame audit, per your rule.

**Why it fails between views:** the AI views were painted over game frames, but the painting moved the geometry. The water is higher and wider, the arrows sit on a rising lane, and the rings are closer. The pixels only land correctly from the exact camera that made them.

**Next specific test (free, local):** rebuild the tunnel interior's water and ring geometry to match the painted views. Depth Anything 3 will measure the shape from V04-1, V04-2 and your approved picture, using their known cameras. Once the geometry matches, both views should agree and the ghosting should go. Depth Anything 3 is reinstalling now (about 5 minutes); no fal or Marble credits will be spent.

## 3. Your question: can I recreate all five screens? Yes or no?

**Not proven yet, so I can't honestly say yes today.**

**A. Matching the approved start view: YES, done on all five.** It uses your own pixels on fitted 3D geometry. I checked each screen's start frame against its painting by eye.

**B. Consistent scenery while Benny swims: NOT YET PROVEN.** Each painting is one camera view; everything Benny swims into afterwards is unseen. Projected pixels smear or tear once the camera moves. Only real 3D geometry plus world-anchored textures cut from your art holds up, and that is being built piece by piece.

**C. 8.5/10 on every screen: NOT YET PROVEN. No screen is there yet while swimming.** Current honest estimates, which are judgments not measurements:
- **08:** about 7–7.5
- **05:** about 6.5–7
- **06:** about 6.5–7
- **07:** about 4–4.5
- **04:** about 3.5

05 and 08 each gained two kept steps this session.

**Realistic AI-only path: yes, plausibly**, with no new paid service needed for the next steps.
- **05 and 08:** most likely to reach 8.5, by continuing the small steps.
- **06:** needs its bending tunnel rebuilt to the measured shape. The rejected bend method stays rejected.
- **04 and 07:** hardest, because the paintings show the least of what Benny swims through. They depend on continuation views you approve, plus geometry fitted to them.

## 4. A better tool?
Not needed for the next step: Depth Anything 3 is already tested and free. I'll only look for a new tool if the geometry fit fails. Stable Virtual Camera and ViewCrafter are still down on Hugging Face's side.

## 5. Everything else
- **Screens 05 and 08:** both specialists are still running. Each has delivered two GOOD IMPROVEMENT steps, all sent to you as separate full-size pictures.
- **Screens 07 and 06:** queued for the next slots.
- **Autosave:** verified. This session's branch is at `0ef79a9`, the Screen 04 branch at `40a72c43`, and both specialist branches match GitHub. Their unfinished edits are snapshotted every five minutes.

## 1. WHAT I UNDERSTAND
You already designed these five screens. Each approved picture is the blueprint, not inspiration. You want the playable game to look like that exact picture: the same scenery, structures, colours, lighting, composition, style and camera feel.

And not only in the first frame. As Benny swims forward and steers, every moment should still look like the same painted world, just seen from a little further in. The pink character stays the same Benny throughout: same look and proportions, believable size, sitting in the water. Nothing may turn into a different, generic or simplified look. A method that only looks right from one camera angle and breaks when Benny moves counts as a failure, and gets replaced by one that holds up in motion.

## 2. WHAT I WILL DO
**Screen 04, Through the Five Arrows** (rebuild with real 3D geometry and materials). The opening keeps your picture: the pink laundry room with porthole, towels and panel, the big gold washer opening with bulb lights, and foam and bubbles around the rim. Inside, as Benny swims:
- polished glossy yellow-gold rings converging to a warm glow
- thick water filling the tunnel floor and climbing about 2 units up the sides (measured from the views), cyan in the middle with broad pink-magenta swirls
- five glowing white-cyan chevron arrows readable ahead of Benny
- soft low foam along the water edges instead of the tall foam-ball walls
- small iridescent bubbles, and the gold coin trail as approved

**Screen 05, Flow Into the Current.** Purple slotted metal walls, gold rings stepping down to the glowing throat, a thick glossy S-river (cyan core, hot-pink satin bands, white glints) and puffy foam clouds on the banks, all holding the whole way through. Still to fix:
- the flat pale wedge on the left far bank
- the visible join line between near and far water
- wall slots too big, with a faint double pattern
- wrong bits at the 05→06 transition: floating coins, a torn wall fragment, rings floating at the hand-over

**Screen 08, Final Spin Chamber.** The chamber stays as approved: gold arch, round washer door with chrome rim and gold bulb ring, perforated purple drum walls, spout cups pouring water, the folded towel on its platform, and the S-river with coins. Still to fix:
- the gold wedge on the upper-right cup
- the hard edge in the top-left corner
- water glints about twice too large
- cup undersides painted flat on the wall behind them

**Queued: Screen 06, Crest the Rinse Arch.** Today the tunnel turns into a straight stack of concentric rings while Benny swims. The target is your sweeping water crest and bending purple-and-gold tunnel continuing as painted. The two rejected bending attempts will not be repeated; any new approach will be genuinely different and justified first.

**Queued: Screen 07, Dive the Washer Tube.** Today it turns into plain wall slabs and grey foam walls. The target is the same purple slotted tube with gold rings and the winding cyan-pink river all the way through the plunge.

## 3. WHAT I WILL NOT DO
- Report technical progress (tools, diagnostics, saves, renders) as if it were visual improvement.
- Keep any method that matches one camera angle but smears, tears, ghosts or pops while Benny moves.
- Let scenery drift: no new elements, no removed elements, no different tunnel shape or layout.
- Let style drift: no different colours, materials, lighting or art style from your picture.
- Replace your approved look with generic assets, procedural patterns or "similar-looking" substitutes.

- Approve anything on your behalf.

## 4. VISUAL SUCCESS STANDARD
- **Scenery match:** at the start view the game frame is indistinguishable at a glance from your picture: same structures in the same places and proportions.
- **Style match:** same colours, glossy gold, purple metal, water formations, foam, glow and lighting, with no flat, blurry or plastic-looking areas.
- **Still matching while playing:** at the beginning, middle and end of the stage and when steering hard left and right, every frame looks like the same painted world seen from inside it. No smears, seams, tears, double images or sudden changes.
- **Benny integrated:** same Benny, believable size, seated in the water with splash and wake, never hidden behind wrong scenery or floating.
- **How it's judged:** by eye, on separate full-size APPROVED / BEFORE / NEW pictures; colour numbers never count as proof. The goal is at least 8.5/10 throughout gameplay, ideally 9–9.5, and only you decide when a screen is approved.
